Huellitas Eternas — paquete HTML corregido v3.2.0
============================================================
CORRECCIONES PRINCIPALES
• Pantalla de carga reconstruida: una sola lógica de progreso y una sola navegación.
• Eliminados los marcadores de plantilla que estaban apareciendo literalmente en las páginas.
• Cada HTML contiene su propio CSS y JavaScript.
• Navegación, tema, menú móvil, progreso, animaciones y formularios con una sola capa de interacción por página.
• Se mantiene el conjunto de 14 páginas de la versión anterior.
• Favicon e imágenes de marca: icon.png.
• Diseño responsive para móvil, tablet, escritorio y pantallas grandes.
• Soporte para reduced motion y navegación por teclado.
• Formularios demo no fingen enviar datos a un servidor.
• Las integraciones reales de QR, cuentas, pagos, correo y almacenamiento quedan preparadas pero no se simulan como si ya existieran.

ORDEN DE PÁGINAS
1. index.html
2. home.html
3. producto.html
4. personalizar.html
5. nosotros.html
6. faq.html
7. contacto.html
8. memorial.html
9. como-funciona.html
10. ayuda.html
11. politica-privacidad.html
12. terminos-condiciones.html
13. cookies.html
14. 404.html

NOTA: las páginas legales son plantillas y deben adaptarse a la operación real antes de publicar.
============================================================

    <div class="orbit" aria-hidden="true"><i></i></div>
    <img class="logo" src="icon.png" alt="Huellitas Eternas">
    <h1>Huellitas Eternas</h1>
    <p id="loadStatus" class="status">Preparando tu experiencia…</p>
    <div class="bar" role="progressbar" aria-label="Cargando" aria-valuemin="0" aria-valuemax="100" aria-valuenow="0"><span id="loadBar"></span></div>
    <div class="loader-meta"><span>Personalización</span><span>Espacio digital</span><span>Recuerdos</span></div>
    <button id="skipLoad" class="skip-load" type="button">Continuar ahora</button>
  </section>

<script>
(()=>{"use strict";
const bar=document.getElementById("loadBar"),status=document.getElementById("loadStatus"),skip=document.getElementById("skipLoad");
const messages=["Preparando tu experiencia…","Cuidando cada detalle…","Preparando tu espacio…","Casi listo…"];
const reduce=matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;
let finished=false,start=performance.now(),duration=4800;
function go(){if(finished)return;finished=true;location.replace("home.html")}
function tick(now){
  const p=Math.min(1,(now-start)/duration);
  const eased=1-Math.pow(1-p,3);
  if(bar)bar.style.width=(eased*100).toFixed(1)+"%";
  if(status)status.textContent=messages[Math.min(messages.length-1,Math.floor(p*messages.length))];
  if(p<1)requestAnimationFrame(tick);else go();
}
skip?.addEventListener("click",go);
window.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.key==="Enter"||e.key===" "||e.key==="Escape"){e.preventDefault();go()}},{once:false});
window.addEventListener("pageshow",()=>{start=performance.now();requestAnimationFrame(tick)},{once:true});
})();
</script>
</body></html>

<script>
(()=>{"use strict";
const $=(s,c=document)=>c.querySelector(s), $$=(s,c=document)=>[...c.querySelectorAll(s)];
const root=document.documentElement, body=document.body;
const reduce=window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;

try{
  const saved=localStorage.getItem("he-theme");
  if(saved==="dark"||saved==="light") root.dataset.theme=saved;
}catch(_){}

const theme=$(".theme-btn");
theme?.addEventListener("click",()=>{
  const next=root.dataset.theme==="dark"?"light":"dark";
  root.dataset.theme=next;
  try{localStorage.setItem("he-theme",next)}catch(_){}
  toast(next==="dark"?"Modo oscuro activado":"Modo claro activado");
});

const menu=$(".menu-btn"), nav=$(".nav"), backdrop=$(".backdrop");
function closeMenu(){nav?.classList.remove("open");menu?.setAttribute("aria-expanded","false");backdrop?.classList.remove("show")}
menu?.addEventListener("click",()=>{
  const open=!nav?.classList.contains("open");
  nav?.classList.toggle("open",open);menu.setAttribute("aria-expanded",String(open));backdrop?.classList.toggle("show",open);
});
backdrop?.addEventListener("click",closeMenu);
$$(".nav a").forEach(a=>a.addEventListener("click",closeMenu));
window.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.key==="Escape"){closeMenu();$("#he-modal")?.classList.remove("show");backdrop?.classList.remove("show")}});

const progress=document.createElement("div");progress.className="progress-scroll";body.append(progress);
function scrollFX(){
  const max=Math.max(0,document.documentElement.scrollHeight-window.innerHeight);
  progress.style.width=(max?(window.scrollY/max)*100:0)+"%";
  $(".site-header")?.classList.toggle("scrolled",window.scrollY>12);
  $("#topBtn")?.classList.toggle("show",window.scrollY>450);
}
window.addEventListener("scroll",scrollFX,{passive:true});scrollFX();
$("#topBtn")?.addEventListener("click",()=>window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:reduce?"auto":"smooth"}));

const current=(location.pathname.split("/").pop()||"index.html").toLowerCase();
$$(".nav a").forEach(a=>{try{
  const target=(new URL(a.href,location.href).pathname.split("/").pop()||"index.html").toLowerCase();
  a.classList.toggle("active",target===current);
}catch(_){}});

if("IntersectionObserver" in window && !reduce){
  const io=new IntersectionObserver(entries=>entries.forEach(entry=>{
    if(entry.isIntersecting){entry.target.classList.add("show");io.unobserve(entry.target)}
  }),{threshold:.08,rootMargin:"0px 0px -35px"});
  $$(".reveal").forEach((el,i)=>{el.style.transitionDelay=Math.min(i*28,240)+"ms";io.observe(el)});
}else $$(".reveal").forEach(el=>el.classList.add("show"));

if(!reduce && window.matchMedia("(pointer:fine)").matches){
  const glow=document.createElement("div");glow.className="cursor-glow";body.append(glow);
  window.addEventListener("pointermove",e=>{glow.style.left=e.clientX+"px";glow.style.top=e.clientY+"px"},{passive:true});
  $$(".visual[data-tilt='true'],.tilt").forEach(el=>{
    el.addEventListener("pointermove",e=>{
      const r=el.getBoundingClientRect(),x=(e.clientX-r.left)/r.width-.5,y=(e.clientY-r.top)/r.height-.5;
      el.style.transform=`perspective(900px) rotateX(${y*-2.4}deg) rotateY(${x*2.4}deg) translateY(-4px)`;
    });
    el.addEventListener("pointerleave",()=>el.style.transform="");
  });
}

if(!reduce){
  const box=document.createElement("div");box.id="particles";
  const count=innerWidth<600?10:20;
  for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
    const p=document.createElement("i");p.className="particle";
    p.style.cssText=`--s:${2+Math.random()*6}px;--x:${Math.random()*100}%;--o:${.06+Math.random()*.16};--d:${11+Math.random()*15}s;--delay:${-Math.random()*20}s`;
    box.append(p);
  }
  body.prepend(box);
}

function toast(message,ms=3000){
  let stack=$(".toast-stack");
  if(!stack){stack=document.createElement("div");stack.className="toast-stack";stack.style.cssText="position:fixed;right:18px;top:88px;z-index:500;display:grid;gap:9px;width:min(360px,calc(100vw - 36px))";body.append(stack)}
  const t=document.createElement("div");
  t.textContent=message;t.style.cssText="padding:13px 15px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:14px;background:var(--surface-solid);color:var(--text);box-shadow:var(--shadow);font-weight:700";
  stack.append(t);setTimeout(()=>{t.remove()},ms);
}
window.heToast=toast;

$$(".btn").forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener("pointerdown",e=>{
  if(reduce)return;
  const r=document.createElement("span"),q=btn.getBoundingClientRect(),s=Math.max(q.width,q.height);
  r.className="ripple";r.style.width=r.style.height=s+"px";r.style.left=(e.clientX-q.left-s/2)+"px";r.style.top=(e.clientY-q.top-s/2)+"px";
  btn.append(r);
  r.animate([{transform:"scale(0)",opacity:.65},{transform:"scale(2.4)",opacity:0}],{duration:600,easing:"ease-out"}).onfinish=()=>r.remove();
}));

$$('a[href^="#"]').forEach(a=>a.addEventListener("click",e=>{
  const target=$(a.getAttribute("href"));if(target){e.preventDefault();target.scrollIntoView({behavior:reduce?"auto":"smooth"})}
}));

$$('a[target="_blank"]').forEach(a=>a.rel="noopener noreferrer");

$$("[data-search-target]").forEach(input=>input.addEventListener("input",()=>{
  const target=$(input.dataset.searchTarget);if(!target)return;
  const q=input.value.trim().toLowerCase();
  $$("details,article,.card",target).forEach(el=>el.hidden=!!q&&!el.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(q));
}));

function animateCounter(el){
  const n=Number(el.dataset.count);if(!Number.isFinite(n))return;
  if(reduce){el.textContent=n.toLocaleString("es-ES");return}
  const start=performance.now(),duration=900;
  const frame=t=>{
    const p=Math.min(1,(t-start)/duration),ease=1-Math.pow(1-p,3);
    el.textContent=Math.round(n*ease).toLocaleString("es-ES");
    if(p<1)requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  };requestAnimationFrame(frame);
}
if("IntersectionObserver" in window && !reduce){
  const io=new IntersectionObserver(es=>es.forEach(e=>{if(e.isIntersecting){animateCounter(e.target);io.unobserve(e.target)}}),{threshold:.5});
  $$("[data-count]").forEach(e=>io.observe(e));
}else $$("[data-count]").forEach(animateCounter);

function openModal(title,content){
  let m=$("#he-modal");
  if(!m){
    m=document.createElement("section");m.id="he-modal";m.className="modal";m.setAttribute("role","dialog");m.setAttribute("aria-modal","true");
    body.append(m);
  }
  m.innerHTML=`<button class="modal-close" type="button" aria-label="Cerrar">×</button><h2>${title}</h2><div>${content}</div>`;
  m.classList.add("show");backdrop?.classList.add("show");m.querySelector(".modal-close")?.focus();
  m.querySelector(".modal-close")?.addEventListener("click",()=>{m.classList.remove("show");backdrop?.classList.remove("show")},{once:true});
}
window.heModal=openModal;

$$("form").forEach(form=>{
  form.addEventListener("invalid",()=>toast("Revisa los campos marcados antes de continuar."),true);
  form.addEventListener("submit",e=>{
    const btn=form.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
    if(btn && btn.dataset.locked==="1"){e.preventDefault();return}
    if(btn){btn.dataset.locked="1";btn.dataset.original=btn.textContent;btn.textContent="Procesando…";btn.disabled=true;
      setTimeout(()=>{btn.disabled=false;btn.dataset.locked="0";btn.textContent=btn.dataset.original||"Enviar"},1600)}
  });
});

const year=$("#year");if(year)year.textContent=new Date().getFullYear();

try{
  if(!localStorage.getItem("he-cookie-choice")){
    const b=document.createElement("aside");b.className="cookie-banner show";b.setAttribute("aria-label","Preferencias de privacidad");
    b.innerHTML=`<div><strong>Preferencias de privacidad</strong><br><span class="muted">Podemos guardar preferencias básicas como el modo de color en este dispositivo.</span></div><div class="cookie-actions"><button class="btn secondary" type="button" data-cookie="necessary">Continuar</button><button class="btn primary" type="button" data-cookie="ok">Aceptar</button></div>`;
    body.append(b);
    b.addEventListener("click",e=>{const x=e.target.closest("[data-cookie]");if(x){localStorage.setItem("he-cookie-choice",x.dataset.cookie);b.remove();toast("Preferencia guardada ✓")}});
  }
}catch(_){}

window.addEventListener("offline",()=>toast("Sin conexión: algunas funciones pueden esperar."));
window.addEventListener("online",()=>toast("Conexión recuperada ✓"));
window.HuellitasEternas={version:"3.1.0",toast,openModal};
})();

<script>
(()=>{"use strict";
const $=(s,c=document)=>c.querySelector(s), $$=(s,c=document)=>[...c.querySelectorAll(s)];
const root=document.documentElement, body=document.body;
const reduce=window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;

try{
  const saved=localStorage.getItem("he-theme");
  if(saved==="dark"||saved==="light") root.dataset.theme=saved;
}catch(_){}

const theme=$(".theme-btn");
theme?.addEventListener("click",()=>{
  const next=root.dataset.theme==="dark"?"light":"dark";
  root.dataset.theme=next;
  try{localStorage.setItem("he-theme",next)}catch(_){}
  toast(next==="dark"?"Modo oscuro activado":"Modo claro activado");
});

const menu=$(".menu-btn"), nav=$(".nav"), backdrop=$(".backdrop");
function closeMenu(){nav?.classList.remove("open");menu?.setAttribute("aria-expanded","false");backdrop?.classList.remove("show")}
menu?.addEventListener("click",()=>{
  const open=!nav?.classList.contains("open");
  nav?.classList.toggle("open",open);menu.setAttribute("aria-expanded",String(open));backdrop?.classList.toggle("show",open);
});
backdrop?.addEventListener("click",closeMenu);
$$(".nav a").forEach(a=>a.addEventListener("click",closeMenu));
window.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.key==="Escape"){closeMenu();$("#he-modal")?.classList.remove("show");backdrop?.classList.remove("show")}});

const progress=document.createElement("div");progress.className="progress-scroll";body.append(progress);
function scrollFX(){
  const max=Math.max(0,document.documentElement.scrollHeight-window.innerHeight);
  progress.style.width=(max?(window.scrollY/max)*100:0)+"%";
  $(".site-header")?.classList.toggle("scrolled",window.scrollY>12);
  $("#topBtn")?.classList.toggle("show",window.scrollY>450);
}
window.addEventListener("scroll",scrollFX,{passive:true});scrollFX();
$("#topBtn")?.addEventListener("click",()=>window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:reduce?"auto":"smooth"}));

const current=(location.pathname.split("/").pop()||"index.html").toLowerCase();
$$(".nav a").forEach(a=>{try{
  const target=(new URL(a.href,location.href).pathname.split("/").pop()||"index.html").toLowerCase();
  a.classList.toggle("active",target===current);
}catch(_){}});

if("IntersectionObserver" in window && !reduce){
  const io=new IntersectionObserver(entries=>entries.forEach(entry=>{
    if(entry.isIntersecting){entry.target.classList.add("show");io.unobserve(entry.target)}
  }),{threshold:.08,rootMargin:"0px 0px -35px"});
  $$(".reveal").forEach((el,i)=>{el.style.transitionDelay=Math.min(i*28,240)+"ms";io.observe(el)});
}else $$(".reveal").forEach(el=>el.classList.add("show"));

if(!reduce && window.matchMedia("(pointer:fine)").matches){
  const glow=document.createElement("div");glow.className="cursor-glow";body.append(glow);
  window.addEventListener("pointermove",e=>{glow.style.left=e.clientX+"px";glow.style.top=e.clientY+"px"},{passive:true});
  $$(".visual[data-tilt='true'],.tilt").forEach(el=>{
    el.addEventListener("pointermove",e=>{
      const r=el.getBoundingClientRect(),x=(e.clientX-r.left)/r.width-.5,y=(e.clientY-r.top)/r.height-.5;
      el.style.transform=`perspective(900px) rotateX(${y*-2.4}deg) rotateY(${x*2.4}deg) translateY(-4px)`;
    });
    el.addEventListener("pointerleave",()=>el.style.transform="");
  });
}

if(!reduce){
  const box=document.createElement("div");box.id="particles";
  const count=innerWidth<600?10:20;
  for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
    const p=document.createElement("i");p.className="particle";
    p.style.cssText=`--s:${2+Math.random()*6}px;--x:${Math.random()*100}%;--o:${.06+Math.random()*.16};--d:${11+Math.random()*15}s;--delay:${-Math.random()*20}s`;
    box.append(p);
  }
  body.prepend(box);
}

function toast(message,ms=3000){
  let stack=$(".toast-stack");
  if(!stack){stack=document.createElement("div");stack.className="toast-stack";stack.style.cssText="position:fixed;right:18px;top:88px;z-index:500;display:grid;gap:9px;width:min(360px,calc(100vw - 36px))";body.append(stack)}
  const t=document.createElement("div");
  t.textContent=message;t.style.cssText="padding:13px 15px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:14px;background:var(--surface-solid);color:var(--text);box-shadow:var(--shadow);font-weight:700";
  stack.append(t);setTimeout(()=>{t.remove()},ms);
}
window.heToast=toast;

$$(".btn").forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener("pointerdown",e=>{
  if(reduce)return;
  const r=document.createElement("span"),q=btn.getBoundingClientRect(),s=Math.max(q.width,q.height);
  r.className="ripple";r.style.width=r.style.height=s+"px";r.style.left=(e.clientX-q.left-s/2)+"px";r.style.top=(e.clientY-q.top-s/2)+"px";
  btn.append(r);
  r.animate([{transform:"scale(0)",opacity:.65},{transform:"scale(2.4)",opacity:0}],{duration:600,easing:"ease-out"}).onfinish=()=>r.remove();
}));

$$('a[href^="#"]').forEach(a=>a.addEventListener("click",e=>{
  const target=$(a.getAttribute("href"));if(target){e.preventDefault();target.scrollIntoView({behavior:reduce?"auto":"smooth"})}
}));

$$('a[target="_blank"]').forEach(a=>a.rel="noopener noreferrer");

$$("[data-search-target]").forEach(input=>input.addEventListener("input",()=>{
  const target=$(input.dataset.searchTarget);if(!target)return;
  const q=input.value.trim().toLowerCase();
  $$("details,article,.card",target).forEach(el=>el.hidden=!!q&&!el.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(q));
}));

function animateCounter(el){
  const n=Number(el.dataset.count);if(!Number.isFinite(n))return;
  if(reduce){el.textContent=n.toLocaleString("es-ES");return}
  const start=performance.now(),duration=900;
  const frame=t=>{
    const p=Math.min(1,(t-start)/duration),ease=1-Math.pow(1-p,3);
    el.textContent=Math.round(n*ease).toLocaleString("es-ES");
    if(p<1)requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  };requestAnimationFrame(frame);
}
if("IntersectionObserver" in window && !reduce){
  const io=new IntersectionObserver(es=>es.forEach(e=>{if(e.isIntersecting){animateCounter(e.target);io.unobserve(e.target)}}),{threshold:.5});
  $$("[data-count]").forEach(e=>io.observe(e));
}else $$("[data-count]").forEach(animateCounter);

function openModal(title,content){
  let m=$("#he-modal");
  if(!m){
    m=document.createElement("section");m.id="he-modal";m.className="modal";m.setAttribute("role","dialog");m.setAttribute("aria-modal","true");
    body.append(m);
  }
  m.innerHTML=`<button class="modal-close" type="button" aria-label="Cerrar">×</button><h2>${title}</h2><div>${content}</div>`;
  m.classList.add("show");backdrop?.classList.add("show");m.querySelector(".modal-close")?.focus();
  m.querySelector(".modal-close")?.addEventListener("click",()=>{m.classList.remove("show");backdrop?.classList.remove("show")},{once:true});
}
window.heModal=openModal;

$$("form").forEach(form=>{
  form.addEventListener("invalid",()=>toast("Revisa los campos marcados antes de continuar."),true);
  form.addEventListener("submit",e=>{
    const btn=form.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
    if(btn && btn.dataset.locked==="1"){e.preventDefault();return}
    if(btn){btn.dataset.locked="1";btn.dataset.original=btn.textContent;btn.textContent="Procesando…";btn.disabled=true;
      setTimeout(()=>{btn.disabled=false;btn.dataset.locked="0";btn.textContent=btn.dataset.original||"Enviar"},1600)}
  });
});

const year=$("#year");if(year)year.textContent=new Date().getFullYear();

try{
  if(!localStorage.getItem("he-cookie-choice")){
    const b=document.createElement("aside");b.className="cookie-banner show";b.setAttribute("aria-label","Preferencias de privacidad");
    b.innerHTML=`<div><strong>Preferencias de privacidad</strong><br><span class="muted">Podemos guardar preferencias básicas como el modo de color en este dispositivo.</span></div><div class="cookie-actions"><button class="btn secondary" type="button" data-cookie="necessary">Continuar</button><button class="btn primary" type="button" data-cookie="ok">Aceptar</button></div>`;
    body.append(b);
    b.addEventListener("click",e=>{const x=e.target.closest("[data-cookie]");if(x){localStorage.setItem("he-cookie-choice",x.dataset.cookie);b.remove();toast("Preferencia guardada ✓")}});
  }
}catch(_){}

window.addEventListener("offline",()=>toast("Sin conexión: algunas funciones pueden esperar."));
window.addEventListener("online",()=>toast("Conexión recuperada ✓"));
window.HuellitasEternas={version:"3.1.0",toast,openModal};
})();

<script>
(()=>{"use strict";
const $=(s,c=document)=>c.querySelector(s), $$=(s,c=document)=>[...c.querySelectorAll(s)];
const root=document.documentElement, body=document.body;
const reduce=window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;

try{
  const saved=localStorage.getItem("he-theme");
  if(saved==="dark"||saved==="light") root.dataset.theme=saved;
}catch(_){}

const theme=$(".theme-btn");
theme?.addEventListener("click",()=>{
  const next=root.dataset.theme==="dark"?"light":"dark";
  root.dataset.theme=next;
  try{localStorage.setItem("he-theme",next)}catch(_){}
  toast(next==="dark"?"Modo oscuro activado":"Modo claro activado");
});

const menu=$(".menu-btn"), nav=$(".nav"), backdrop=$(".backdrop");
function closeMenu(){nav?.classList.remove("open");menu?.setAttribute("aria-expanded","false");backdrop?.classList.remove("show")}
menu?.addEventListener("click",()=>{
  const open=!nav?.classList.contains("open");
  nav?.classList.toggle("open",open);menu.setAttribute("aria-expanded",String(open));backdrop?.classList.toggle("show",open);
});
backdrop?.addEventListener("click",closeMenu);
$$(".nav a").forEach(a=>a.addEventListener("click",closeMenu));
window.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.key==="Escape"){closeMenu();$("#he-modal")?.classList.remove("show");backdrop?.classList.remove("show")}});

const progress=document.createElement("div");progress.className="progress-scroll";body.append(progress);
function scrollFX(){
  const max=Math.max(0,document.documentElement.scrollHeight-window.innerHeight);
  progress.style.width=(max?(window.scrollY/max)*100:0)+"%";
  $(".site-header")?.classList.toggle("scrolled",window.scrollY>12);
  $("#topBtn")?.classList.toggle("show",window.scrollY>450);
}
window.addEventListener("scroll",scrollFX,{passive:true});scrollFX();
$("#topBtn")?.addEventListener("click",()=>window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:reduce?"auto":"smooth"}));

const current=(location.pathname.split("/").pop()||"index.html").toLowerCase();
$$(".nav a").forEach(a=>{try{
  const target=(new URL(a.href,location.href).pathname.split("/").pop()||"index.html").toLowerCase();
  a.classList.toggle("active",target===current);
}catch(_){}});

if("IntersectionObserver" in window && !reduce){
  const io=new IntersectionObserver(entries=>entries.forEach(entry=>{
    if(entry.isIntersecting){entry.target.classList.add("show");io.unobserve(entry.target)}
  }),{threshold:.08,rootMargin:"0px 0px -35px"});
  $$(".reveal").forEach((el,i)=>{el.style.transitionDelay=Math.min(i*28,240)+"ms";io.observe(el)});
}else $$(".reveal").forEach(el=>el.classList.add("show"));

if(!reduce && window.matchMedia("(pointer:fine)").matches){
  const glow=document.createElement("div");glow.className="cursor-glow";body.append(glow);
  window.addEventListener("pointermove",e=>{glow.style.left=e.clientX+"px";glow.style.top=e.clientY+"px"},{passive:true});
  $$(".visual[data-tilt='true'],.tilt").forEach(el=>{
    el.addEventListener("pointermove",e=>{
      const r=el.getBoundingClientRect(),x=(e.clientX-r.left)/r.width-.5,y=(e.clientY-r.top)/r.height-.5;
      el.style.transform=`perspective(900px) rotateX(${y*-2.4}deg) rotateY(${x*2.4}deg) translateY(-4px)`;
    });
    el.addEventListener("pointerleave",()=>el.style.transform="");
  });
}

if(!reduce){
  const box=document.createElement("div");box.id="particles";
  const count=innerWidth<600?10:20;
  for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
    const p=document.createElement("i");p.className="particle";
    p.style.cssText=`--s:${2+Math.random()*6}px;--x:${Math.random()*100}%;--o:${.06+Math.random()*.16};--d:${11+Math.random()*15}s;--delay:${-Math.random()*20}s`;
    box.append(p);
  }
  body.prepend(box);
}

function toast(message,ms=3000){
  let stack=$(".toast-stack");
  if(!stack){stack=document.createElement("div");stack.className="toast-stack";stack.style.cssText="position:fixed;right:18px;top:88px;z-index:500;display:grid;gap:9px;width:min(360px,calc(100vw - 36px))";body.append(stack)}
  const t=document.createElement("div");
  t.textContent=message;t.style.cssText="padding:13px 15px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:14px;background:var(--surface-solid);color:var(--text);box-shadow:var(--shadow);font-weight:700";
  stack.append(t);setTimeout(()=>{t.remove()},ms);
}
window.heToast=toast;

$$(".btn").forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener("pointerdown",e=>{
  if(reduce)return;
  const r=document.createElement("span"),q=btn.getBoundingClientRect(),s=Math.max(q.width,q.height);
  r.className="ripple";r.style.width=r.style.height=s+"px";r.style.left=(e.clientX-q.left-s/2)+"px";r.style.top=(e.clientY-q.top-s/2)+"px";
  btn.append(r);
  r.animate([{transform:"scale(0)",opacity:.65},{transform:"scale(2.4)",opacity:0}],{duration:600,easing:"ease-out"}).onfinish=()=>r.remove();
}));

$$('a[href^="#"]').forEach(a=>a.addEventListener("click",e=>{
  const target=$(a.getAttribute("href"));if(target){e.preventDefault();target.scrollIntoView({behavior:reduce?"auto":"smooth"})}
}));

$$('a[target="_blank"]').forEach(a=>a.rel="noopener noreferrer");

$$("[data-search-target]").forEach(input=>input.addEventListener("input",()=>{
  const target=$(input.dataset.searchTarget);if(!target)return;
  const q=input.value.trim().toLowerCase();
  $$("details,article,.card",target).forEach(el=>el.hidden=!!q&&!el.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(q));
}));

function animateCounter(el){
  const n=Number(el.dataset.count);if(!Number.isFinite(n))return;
  if(reduce){el.textContent=n.toLocaleString("es-ES");return}
  const start=performance.now(),duration=900;
  const frame=t=>{
    const p=Math.min(1,(t-start)/duration),ease=1-Math.pow(1-p,3);
    el.textContent=Math.round(n*ease).toLocaleString("es-ES");
    if(p<1)requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  };requestAnimationFrame(frame);
}
if("IntersectionObserver" in window && !reduce){
  const io=new IntersectionObserver(es=>es.forEach(e=>{if(e.isIntersecting){animateCounter(e.target);io.unobserve(e.target)}}),{threshold:.5});
  $$("[data-count]").forEach(e=>io.observe(e));
}else $$("[data-count]").forEach(animateCounter);

function openModal(title,content){
  let m=$("#he-modal");
  if(!m){
    m=document.createElement("section");m.id="he-modal";m.className="modal";m.setAttribute("role","dialog");m.setAttribute("aria-modal","true");
    body.append(m);
  }
  m.innerHTML=`<button class="modal-close" type="button" aria-label="Cerrar">×</button><h2>${title}</h2><div>${content}</div>`;
  m.classList.add("show");backdrop?.classList.add("show");m.querySelector(".modal-close")?.focus();
  m.querySelector(".modal-close")?.addEventListener("click",()=>{m.classList.remove("show");backdrop?.classList.remove("show")},{once:true});
}
window.heModal=openModal;

$$("form").forEach(form=>{
  form.addEventListener("invalid",()=>toast("Revisa los campos marcados antes de continuar."),true);
  form.addEventListener("submit",e=>{
    const btn=form.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
    if(btn && btn.dataset.locked==="1"){e.preventDefault();return}
    if(btn){btn.dataset.locked="1";btn.dataset.original=btn.textContent;btn.textContent="Procesando…";btn.disabled=true;
      setTimeout(()=>{btn.disabled=false;btn.dataset.locked="0";btn.textContent=btn.dataset.original||"Enviar"},1600)}
  });
});

const year=$("#year");if(year)year.textContent=new Date().getFullYear();

try{
  if(!localStorage.getItem("he-cookie-choice")){
    const b=document.createElement("aside");b.className="cookie-banner show";b.setAttribute("aria-label","Preferencias de privacidad");
    b.innerHTML=`<div><strong>Preferencias de privacidad</strong><br><span class="muted">Podemos guardar preferencias básicas como el modo de color en este dispositivo.</span></div><div class="cookie-actions"><button class="btn secondary" type="button" data-cookie="necessary">Continuar</button><button class="btn primary" type="button" data-cookie="ok">Aceptar</button></div>`;
    body.append(b);
    b.addEventListener("click",e=>{const x=e.target.closest("[data-cookie]");if(x){localStorage.setItem("he-cookie-choice",x.dataset.cookie);b.remove();toast("Preferencia guardada ✓")}});
  }
}catch(_){}

window.addEventListener("offline",()=>toast("Sin conexión: algunas funciones pueden esperar."));
window.addEventListener("online",()=>toast("Conexión recuperada ✓"));
window.HuellitasEternas={version:"3.1.0",toast,openModal};
})();
</script>

<script>
(()=>{"use strict";
const $=(s,c=document)=>c.querySelector(s), $$=(s,c=document)=>[...c.querySelectorAll(s)];
const root=document.documentElement, body=document.body;
const reduce=window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;

try{
  const saved=localStorage.getItem("he-theme");
  if(saved==="dark"||saved==="light") root.dataset.theme=saved;
}catch(_){}

const theme=$(".theme-btn");
theme?.addEventListener("click",()=>{
  const next=root.dataset.theme==="dark"?"light":"dark";
  root.dataset.theme=next;
  try{localStorage.setItem("he-theme",next)}catch(_){}
  toast(next==="dark"?"Modo oscuro activado":"Modo claro activado");
});

const menu=$(".menu-btn"), nav=$(".nav"), backdrop=$(".backdrop");
function closeMenu(){nav?.classList.remove("open");menu?.setAttribute("aria-expanded","false");backdrop?.classList.remove("show")}
menu?.addEventListener("click",()=>{
  const open=!nav?.classList.contains("open");
  nav?.classList.toggle("open",open);menu.setAttribute("aria-expanded",String(open));backdrop?.classList.toggle("show",open);
});
backdrop?.addEventListener("click",closeMenu);
$$(".nav a").forEach(a=>a.addEventListener("click",closeMenu));
window.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.key==="Escape"){closeMenu();$("#he-modal")?.classList.remove("show");backdrop?.classList.remove("show")}});

const progress=document.createElement("div");progress.className="progress-scroll";body.append(progress);
function scrollFX(){
  const max=Math.max(0,document.documentElement.scrollHeight-window.innerHeight);
  progress.style.width=(max?(window.scrollY/max)*100:0)+"%";
  $(".site-header")?.classList.toggle("scrolled",window.scrollY>12);
  $("#topBtn")?.classList.toggle("show",window.scrollY>450);
}
window.addEventListener("scroll",scrollFX,{passive:true});scrollFX();
$("#topBtn")?.addEventListener("click",()=>window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:reduce?"auto":"smooth"}));

const current=(location.pathname.split("/").pop()||"index.html").toLowerCase();
$$(".nav a").forEach(a=>{try{
  const target=(new URL(a.href,location.href).pathname.split("/").pop()||"index.html").toLowerCase();
  a.classList.toggle("active",target===current);
}catch(_){}});

if("IntersectionObserver" in window && !reduce){
  const io=new IntersectionObserver(entries=>entries.forEach(entry=>{
    if(entry.isIntersecting){entry.target.classList.add("show");io.unobserve(entry.target)}
  }),{threshold:.08,rootMargin:"0px 0px -35px"});
  $$(".reveal").forEach((el,i)=>{el.style.transitionDelay=Math.min(i*28,240)+"ms";io.observe(el)});
}else $$(".reveal").forEach(el=>el.classList.add("show"));

if(!reduce && window.matchMedia("(pointer:fine)").matches){
  const glow=document.createElement("div");glow.className="cursor-glow";body.append(glow);
  window.addEventListener("pointermove",e=>{glow.style.left=e.clientX+"px";glow.style.top=e.clientY+"px"},{passive:true});
  $$(".visual[data-tilt='true'],.tilt").forEach(el=>{
    el.addEventListener("pointermove",e=>{
      const r=el.getBoundingClientRect(),x=(e.clientX-r.left)/r.width-.5,y=(e.clientY-r.top)/r.height-.5;
      el.style.transform=`perspective(900px) rotateX(${y*-2.4}deg) rotateY(${x*2.4}deg) translateY(-4px)`;
    });
    el.addEventListener("pointerleave",()=>el.style.transform="");
  });
}

if(!reduce){
  const box=document.createElement("div");box.id="particles";
  const count=innerWidth<600?10:20;
  for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
    const p=document.createElement("i");p.className="particle";
    p.style.cssText=`--s:${2+Math.random()*6}px;--x:${Math.random()*100}%;--o:${.06+Math.random()*.16};--d:${11+Math.random()*15}s;--delay:${-Math.random()*20}s`;
    box.append(p);
  }
  body.prepend(box);
}

function toast(message,ms=3000){
  let stack=$(".toast-stack");
  if(!stack){stack=document.createElement("div");stack.className="toast-stack";stack.style.cssText="position:fixed;right:18px;top:88px;z-index:500;display:grid;gap:9px;width:min(360px,calc(100vw - 36px))";body.append(stack)}
  const t=document.createElement("div");
  t.textContent=message;t.style.cssText="padding:13px 15px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:14px;background:var(--surface-solid);color:var(--text);box-shadow:var(--shadow);font-weight:700";
  stack.append(t);setTimeout(()=>{t.remove()},ms);
}
window.heToast=toast;

$$(".btn").forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener("pointerdown",e=>{
  if(reduce)return;
  const r=document.createElement("span"),q=btn.getBoundingClientRect(),s=Math.max(q.width,q.height);
  r.className="ripple";r.style.width=r.style.height=s+"px";r.style.left=(e.clientX-q.left-s/2)+"px";r.style.top=(e.clientY-q.top-s/2)+"px";
  btn.append(r);
  r.animate([{transform:"scale(0)",opacity:.65},{transform:"scale(2.4)",opacity:0}],{duration:600,easing:"ease-out"}).onfinish=()=>r.remove();
}));

$$('a[href^="#"]').forEach(a=>a.addEventListener("click",e=>{
  const target=$(a.getAttribute("href"));if(target){e.preventDefault();target.scrollIntoView({behavior:reduce?"auto":"smooth"})}
}));

$$('a[target="_blank"]').forEach(a=>a.rel="noopener noreferrer");

$$("[data-search-target]").forEach(input=>input.addEventListener("input",()=>{
  const target=$(input.dataset.searchTarget);if(!target)return;
  const q=input.value.trim().toLowerCase();
  $$("details,article,.card",target).forEach(el=>el.hidden=!!q&&!el.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(q));
}));

function animateCounter(el){
  const n=Number(el.dataset.count);if(!Number.isFinite(n))return;
  if(reduce){el.textContent=n.toLocaleString("es-ES");return}
  const start=performance.now(),duration=900;
  const frame=t=>{
    const p=Math.min(1,(t-start)/duration),ease=1-Math.pow(1-p,3);
    el.textContent=Math.round(n*ease).toLocaleString("es-ES");
    if(p<1)requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  };requestAnimationFrame(frame);
}
if("IntersectionObserver" in window && !reduce){
  const io=new IntersectionObserver(es=>es.forEach(e=>{if(e.isIntersecting){animateCounter(e.target);io.unobserve(e.target)}}),{threshold:.5});
  $$("[data-count]").forEach(e=>io.observe(e));
}else $$("[data-count]").forEach(animateCounter);

function openModal(title,content){
  let m=$("#he-modal");
  if(!m){
    m=document.createElement("section");m.id="he-modal";m.className="modal";m.setAttribute("role","dialog");m.setAttribute("aria-modal","true");
    body.append(m);
  }
  m.innerHTML=`<button class="modal-close" type="button" aria-label="Cerrar">×</button><h2>${title}</h2><div>${content}</div>`;
  m.classList.add("show");backdrop?.classList.add("show");m.querySelector(".modal-close")?.focus();
  m.querySelector(".modal-close")?.addEventListener("click",()=>{m.classList.remove("show");backdrop?.classList.remove("show")},{once:true});
}
window.heModal=openModal;

$$("form").forEach(form=>{
  form.addEventListener("invalid",()=>toast("Revisa los campos marcados antes de continuar."),true);
  form.addEventListener("submit",e=>{
    const btn=form.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
    if(btn && btn.dataset.locked==="1"){e.preventDefault();return}
    if(btn){btn.dataset.locked="1";btn.dataset.original=btn.textContent;btn.textContent="Procesando…";btn.disabled=true;
      setTimeout(()=>{btn.disabled=false;btn.dataset.locked="0";btn.textContent=btn.dataset.original||"Enviar"},1600)}
  });
});

const year=$("#year");if(year)year.textContent=new Date().getFullYear();

try{
  if(!localStorage.getItem("he-cookie-choice")){
    const b=document.createElement("aside");b.className="cookie-banner show";b.setAttribute("aria-label","Preferencias de privacidad");
    b.innerHTML=`<div><strong>Preferencias de privacidad</strong><br><span class="muted">Podemos guardar preferencias básicas como el modo de color en este dispositivo.</span></div><div class="cookie-actions"><button class="btn secondary" type="button" data-cookie="necessary">Continuar</button><button class="btn primary" type="button" data-cookie="ok">Aceptar</button></div>`;
    body.append(b);
    b.addEventListener("click",e=>{const x=e.target.closest("[data-cookie]");if(x){localStorage.setItem("he-cookie-choice",x.dataset.cookie);b.remove();toast("Preferencia guardada ✓")}});
  }
}catch(_){}

window.addEventListener("offline",()=>toast("Sin conexión: algunas funciones pueden esperar."));
window.addEventListener("online",()=>toast("Conexión recuperada ✓"));
window.HuellitasEternas={version:"3.1.0",toast,openModal};
})();
</script>

<script>
(()=>{"use strict";
const $=(s,c=document)=>c.querySelector(s), $$=(s,c=document)=>[...c.querySelectorAll(s)];
const root=document.documentElement, body=document.body;
const reduce=window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;

try{
  const saved=localStorage.getItem("he-theme");
  if(saved==="dark"||saved==="light") root.dataset.theme=saved;
}catch(_){}

const theme=$(".theme-btn");
theme?.addEventListener("click",()=>{
  const next=root.dataset.theme==="dark"?"light":"dark";
  root.dataset.theme=next;
  try{localStorage.setItem("he-theme",next)}catch(_){}
  toast(next==="dark"?"Modo oscuro activado":"Modo claro activado");
});

const menu=$(".menu-btn"), nav=$(".nav"), backdrop=$(".backdrop");
function closeMenu(){nav?.classList.remove("open");menu?.setAttribute("aria-expanded","false");backdrop?.classList.remove("show")}
menu?.addEventListener("click",()=>{
  const open=!nav?.classList.contains("open");
  nav?.classList.toggle("open",open);menu.setAttribute("aria-expanded",String(open));backdrop?.classList.toggle("show",open);
});
backdrop?.addEventListener("click",closeMenu);
$$(".nav a").forEach(a=>a.addEventListener("click",closeMenu));
window.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.key==="Escape"){closeMenu();$("#he-modal")?.classList.remove("show");backdrop?.classList.remove("show")}});

const progress=document.createElement("div");progress.className="progress-scroll";body.append(progress);
function scrollFX(){
  const max=Math.max(0,document.documentElement.scrollHeight-window.innerHeight);
  progress.style.width=(max?(window.scrollY/max)*100:0)+"%";
  $(".site-header")?.classList.toggle("scrolled",window.scrollY>12);
  $("#topBtn")?.classList.toggle("show",window.scrollY>450);
}
window.addEventListener("scroll",scrollFX,{passive:true});scrollFX();
$("#topBtn")?.addEventListener("click",()=>window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:reduce?"auto":"smooth"}));

const current=(location.pathname.split("/").pop()||"index.html").toLowerCase();
$$(".nav a").forEach(a=>{try{
  const target=(new URL(a.href,location.href).pathname.split("/").pop()||"index.html").toLowerCase();
  a.classList.toggle("active",target===current);
}catch(_){}});

if("IntersectionObserver" in window && !reduce){
  const io=new IntersectionObserver(entries=>entries.forEach(entry=>{
    if(entry.isIntersecting){entry.target.classList.add("show");io.unobserve(entry.target)}
  }),{threshold:.08,rootMargin:"0px 0px -35px"});
  $$(".reveal").forEach((el,i)=>{el.style.transitionDelay=Math.min(i*28,240)+"ms";io.observe(el)});
}else $$(".reveal").forEach(el=>el.classList.add("show"));

if(!reduce && window.matchMedia("(pointer:fine)").matches){
  const glow=document.createElement("div");glow.className="cursor-glow";body.append(glow);
  window.addEventListener("pointermove",e=>{glow.style.left=e.clientX+"px";glow.style.top=e.clientY+"px"},{passive:true});
  $$(".visual[data-tilt='true'],.tilt").forEach(el=>{
    el.addEventListener("pointermove",e=>{
      const r=el.getBoundingClientRect(),x=(e.clientX-r.left)/r.width-.5,y=(e.clientY-r.top)/r.height-.5;
      el.style.transform=`perspective(900px) rotateX(${y*-2.4}deg) rotateY(${x*2.4}deg) translateY(-4px)`;
    });
    el.addEventListener("pointerleave",()=>el.style.transform="");
  });
}

if(!reduce){
  const box=document.createElement("div");box.id="particles";
  const count=innerWidth<600?10:20;
  for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
    const p=document.createElement("i");p.className="particle";
    p.style.cssText=`--s:${2+Math.random()*6}px;--x:${Math.random()*100}%;--o:${.06+Math.random()*.16};--d:${11+Math.random()*15}s;--delay:${-Math.random()*20}s`;
    box.append(p);
  }
  body.prepend(box);
}

function toast(message,ms=3000){
  let stack=$(".toast-stack");
  if(!stack){stack=document.createElement("div");stack.className="toast-stack";stack.style.cssText="position:fixed;right:18px;top:88px;z-index:500;display:grid;gap:9px;width:min(360px,calc(100vw - 36px))";body.append(stack)}
  const t=document.createElement("div");
  t.textContent=message;t.style.cssText="padding:13px 15px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:14px;background:var(--surface-solid);color:var(--text);box-shadow:var(--shadow);font-weight:700";
  stack.append(t);setTimeout(()=>{t.remove()},ms);
}
window.heToast=toast;

$$(".btn").forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener("pointerdown",e=>{
  if(reduce)return;
  const r=document.createElement("span"),q=btn.getBoundingClientRect(),s=Math.max(q.width,q.height);
  r.className="ripple";r.style.width=r.style.height=s+"px";r.style.left=(e.clientX-q.left-s/2)+"px";r.style.top=(e.clientY-q.top-s/2)+"px";
  btn.append(r);
  r.animate([{transform:"scale(0)",opacity:.65},{transform:"scale(2.4)",opacity:0}],{duration:600,easing:"ease-out"}).onfinish=()=>r.remove();
}));

$$('a[href^="#"]').forEach(a=>a.addEventListener("click",e=>{
  const target=$(a.getAttribute("href"));if(target){e.preventDefault();target.scrollIntoView({behavior:reduce?"auto":"smooth"})}
}));

$$('a[target="_blank"]').forEach(a=>a.rel="noopener noreferrer");

$$("[data-search-target]").forEach(input=>input.addEventListener("input",()=>{
  const target=$(input.dataset.searchTarget);if(!target)return;
  const q=input.value.trim().toLowerCase();
  $$("details,article,.card",target).forEach(el=>el.hidden=!!q&&!el.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(q));
}));

function animateCounter(el){
  const n=Number(el.dataset.count);if(!Number.isFinite(n))return;
  if(reduce){el.textContent=n.toLocaleString("es-ES");return}
  const start=performance.now(),duration=900;
  const frame=t=>{
    const p=Math.min(1,(t-start)/duration),ease=1-Math.pow(1-p,3);
    el.textContent=Math.round(n*ease).toLocaleString("es-ES");
    if(p<1)requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  };requestAnimationFrame(frame);
}
if("IntersectionObserver" in window && !reduce){
  const io=new IntersectionObserver(es=>es.forEach(e=>{if(e.isIntersecting){animateCounter(e.target);io.unobserve(e.target)}}),{threshold:.5});
  $$("[data-count]").forEach(e=>io.observe(e));
}else $$("[data-count]").forEach(animateCounter);

function openModal(title,content){
  let m=$("#he-modal");
  if(!m){
    m=document.createElement("section");m.id="he-modal";m.className="modal";m.setAttribute("role","dialog");m.setAttribute("aria-modal","true");
    body.append(m);
  }
  m.innerHTML=`<button class="modal-close" type="button" aria-label="Cerrar">×</button><h2>${title}</h2><div>${content}</div>`;
  m.classList.add("show");backdrop?.classList.add("show");m.querySelector(".modal-close")?.focus();
  m.querySelector(".modal-close")?.addEventListener("click",()=>{m.classList.remove("show");backdrop?.classList.remove("show")},{once:true});
}
window.heModal=openModal;

$$("form").forEach(form=>{
  form.addEventListener("invalid",()=>toast("Revisa los campos marcados antes de continuar."),true);
  form.addEventListener("submit",e=>{
    const btn=form.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
    if(btn && btn.dataset.locked==="1"){e.preventDefault();return}
    if(btn){btn.dataset.locked="1";btn.dataset.original=btn.textContent;btn.textContent="Procesando…";btn.disabled=true;
      setTimeout(()=>{btn.disabled=false;btn.dataset.locked="0";btn.textContent=btn.dataset.original||"Enviar"},1600)}
  });
});

const year=$("#year");if(year)year.textContent=new Date().getFullYear();

try{
  if(!localStorage.getItem("he-cookie-choice")){
    const b=document.createElement("aside");b.className="cookie-banner show";b.setAttribute("aria-label","Preferencias de privacidad");
    b.innerHTML=`<div><strong>Preferencias de privacidad</strong><br><span class="muted">Podemos guardar preferencias básicas como el modo de color en este dispositivo.</span></div><div class="cookie-actions"><button class="btn secondary" type="button" data-cookie="necessary">Continuar</button><button class="btn primary" type="button" data-cookie="ok">Aceptar</button></div>`;
    body.append(b);
    b.addEventListener("click",e=>{const x=e.target.closest("[data-cookie]");if(x){localStorage.setItem("he-cookie-choice",x.dataset.cookie);b.remove();toast("Preferencia guardada ✓")}});
  }
}catch(_){}

window.addEventListener("offline",()=>toast("Sin conexión: algunas funciones pueden esperar."));
window.addEventListener("online",()=>toast("Conexión recuperada ✓"));
window.HuellitasEternas={version:"3.1.0",toast,openModal};
})();

<script>
(()=>{"use strict";
const $=(s,c=document)=>c.querySelector(s), $$=(s,c=document)=>[...c.querySelectorAll(s)];
const root=document.documentElement, body=document.body;
const reduce=window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;

try{
  const saved=localStorage.getItem("he-theme");
  if(saved==="dark"||saved==="light") root.dataset.theme=saved;
}catch(_){}

const theme=$(".theme-btn");
theme?.addEventListener("click",()=>{
  const next=root.dataset.theme==="dark"?"light":"dark";
  root.dataset.theme=next;
  try{localStorage.setItem("he-theme",next)}catch(_){}
  toast(next==="dark"?"Modo oscuro activado":"Modo claro activado");
});

const menu=$(".menu-btn"), nav=$(".nav"), backdrop=$(".backdrop");
function closeMenu(){nav?.classList.remove("open");menu?.setAttribute("aria-expanded","false");backdrop?.classList.remove("show")}
menu?.addEventListener("click",()=>{
  const open=!nav?.classList.contains("open");
  nav?.classList.toggle("open",open);menu.setAttribute("aria-expanded",String(open));backdrop?.classList.toggle("show",open);
});
backdrop?.addEventListener("click",closeMenu);
$$(".nav a").forEach(a=>a.addEventListener("click",closeMenu));
window.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.key==="Escape"){closeMenu();$("#he-modal")?.classList.remove("show");backdrop?.classList.remove("show")}});

const progress=document.createElement("div");progress.className="progress-scroll";body.append(progress);
function scrollFX(){
  const max=Math.max(0,document.documentElement.scrollHeight-window.innerHeight);
  progress.style.width=(max?(window.scrollY/max)*100:0)+"%";
  $(".site-header")?.classList.toggle("scrolled",window.scrollY>12);
  $("#topBtn")?.classList.toggle("show",window.scrollY>450);
}
window.addEventListener("scroll",scrollFX,{passive:true});scrollFX();
$("#topBtn")?.addEventListener("click",()=>window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:reduce?"auto":"smooth"}));

const current=(location.pathname.split("/").pop()||"index.html").toLowerCase();
$$(".nav a").forEach(a=>{try{
  const target=(new URL(a.href,location.href).pathname.split("/").pop()||"index.html").toLowerCase();
  a.classList.toggle("active",target===current);
}catch(_){}});

if("IntersectionObserver" in window && !reduce){
  const io=new IntersectionObserver(entries=>entries.forEach(entry=>{
    if(entry.isIntersecting){entry.target.classList.add("show");io.unobserve(entry.target)}
  }),{threshold:.08,rootMargin:"0px 0px -35px"});
  $$(".reveal").forEach((el,i)=>{el.style.transitionDelay=Math.min(i*28,240)+"ms";io.observe(el)});
}else $$(".reveal").forEach(el=>el.classList.add("show"));

if(!reduce && window.matchMedia("(pointer:fine)").matches){
  const glow=document.createElement("div");glow.className="cursor-glow";body.append(glow);
  window.addEventListener("pointermove",e=>{glow.style.left=e.clientX+"px";glow.style.top=e.clientY+"px"},{passive:true});
  $$(".visual[data-tilt='true'],.tilt").forEach(el=>{
    el.addEventListener("pointermove",e=>{
      const r=el.getBoundingClientRect(),x=(e.clientX-r.left)/r.width-.5,y=(e.clientY-r.top)/r.height-.5;
      el.style.transform=`perspective(900px) rotateX(${y*-2.4}deg) rotateY(${x*2.4}deg) translateY(-4px)`;
    });
    el.addEventListener("pointerleave",()=>el.style.transform="");
  });
}

if(!reduce){
  const box=document.createElement("div");box.id="particles";
  const count=innerWidth<600?10:20;
  for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
    const p=document.createElement("i");p.className="particle";
    p.style.cssText=`--s:${2+Math.random()*6}px;--x:${Math.random()*100}%;--o:${.06+Math.random()*.16};--d:${11+Math.random()*15}s;--delay:${-Math.random()*20}s`;
    box.append(p);
  }
  body.prepend(box);
}

function toast(message,ms=3000){
  let stack=$(".toast-stack");
  if(!stack){stack=document.createElement("div");stack.className="toast-stack";stack.style.cssText="position:fixed;right:18px;top:88px;z-index:500;display:grid;gap:9px;width:min(360px,calc(100vw - 36px))";body.append(stack)}
  const t=document.createElement("div");
  t.textContent=message;t.style.cssText="padding:13px 15px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:14px;background:var(--surface-solid);color:var(--text);box-shadow:var(--shadow);font-weight:700";
  stack.append(t);setTimeout(()=>{t.remove()},ms);
}
window.heToast=toast;

$$(".btn").forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener("pointerdown",e=>{
  if(reduce)return;
  const r=document.createElement("span"),q=btn.getBoundingClientRect(),s=Math.max(q.width,q.height);
  r.className="ripple";r.style.width=r.style.height=s+"px";r.style.left=(e.clientX-q.left-s/2)+"px";r.style.top=(e.clientY-q.top-s/2)+"px";
  btn.append(r);
  r.animate([{transform:"scale(0)",opacity:.65},{transform:"scale(2.4)",opacity:0}],{duration:600,easing:"ease-out"}).onfinish=()=>r.remove();
}));

$$('a[href^="#"]').forEach(a=>a.addEventListener("click",e=>{
  const target=$(a.getAttribute("href"));if(target){e.preventDefault();target.scrollIntoView({behavior:reduce?"auto":"smooth"})}
}));

$$('a[target="_blank"]').forEach(a=>a.rel="noopener noreferrer");

$$("[data-search-target]").forEach(input=>input.addEventListener("input",()=>{
  const target=$(input.dataset.searchTarget);if(!target)return;
  const q=input.value.trim().toLowerCase();
  $$("details,article,.card",target).forEach(el=>el.hidden=!!q&&!el.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(q));
}));

function animateCounter(el){
  const n=Number(el.dataset.count);if(!Number.isFinite(n))return;
  if(reduce){el.textContent=n.toLocaleString("es-ES");return}
  const start=performance.now(),duration=900;
  const frame=t=>{
    const p=Math.min(1,(t-start)/duration),ease=1-Math.pow(1-p,3);
    el.textContent=Math.round(n*ease).toLocaleString("es-ES");
    if(p<1)requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  };requestAnimationFrame(frame);
}
if("IntersectionObserver" in window && !reduce){
  const io=new IntersectionObserver(es=>es.forEach(e=>{if(e.isIntersecting){animateCounter(e.target);io.unobserve(e.target)}}),{threshold:.5});
  $$("[data-count]").forEach(e=>io.observe(e));
}else $$("[data-count]").forEach(animateCounter);

function openModal(title,content){
  let m=$("#he-modal");
  if(!m){
    m=document.createElement("section");m.id="he-modal";m.className="modal";m.setAttribute("role","dialog");m.setAttribute("aria-modal","true");
    body.append(m);
  }
  m.innerHTML=`<button class="modal-close" type="button" aria-label="Cerrar">×</button><h2>${title}</h2><div>${content}</div>`;
  m.classList.add("show");backdrop?.classList.add("show");m.querySelector(".modal-close")?.focus();
  m.querySelector(".modal-close")?.addEventListener("click",()=>{m.classList.remove("show");backdrop?.classList.remove("show")},{once:true});
}
window.heModal=openModal;

$$("form").forEach(form=>{
  form.addEventListener("invalid",()=>toast("Revisa los campos marcados antes de continuar."),true);
  form.addEventListener("submit",e=>{
    const btn=form.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
    if(btn && btn.dataset.locked==="1"){e.preventDefault();return}
    if(btn){btn.dataset.locked="1";btn.dataset.original=btn.textContent;btn.textContent="Procesando…";btn.disabled=true;
      setTimeout(()=>{btn.disabled=false;btn.dataset.locked="0";btn.textContent=btn.dataset.original||"Enviar"},1600)}
  });
});

const year=$("#year");if(year)year.textContent=new Date().getFullYear();

try{
  if(!localStorage.getItem("he-cookie-choice")){
    const b=document.createElement("aside");b.className="cookie-banner show";b.setAttribute("aria-label","Preferencias de privacidad");
    b.innerHTML=`<div><strong>Preferencias de privacidad</strong><br><span class="muted">Podemos guardar preferencias básicas como el modo de color en este dispositivo.</span></div><div class="cookie-actions"><button class="btn secondary" type="button" data-cookie="necessary">Continuar</button><button class="btn primary" type="button" data-cookie="ok">Aceptar</button></div>`;
    body.append(b);
    b.addEventListener("click",e=>{const x=e.target.closest("[data-cookie]");if(x){localStorage.setItem("he-cookie-choice",x.dataset.cookie);b.remove();toast("Preferencia guardada ✓")}});
  }
}catch(_){}

window.addEventListener("offline",()=>toast("Sin conexión: algunas funciones pueden esperar."));
window.addEventListener("online",()=>toast("Conexión recuperada ✓"));
window.HuellitasEternas={version:"3.1.0",toast,openModal};
})();
</script>
</body></html>

<script>
(()=>{"use strict";
const $=(s,c=document)=>c.querySelector(s), $$=(s,c=document)=>[...c.querySelectorAll(s)];
const root=document.documentElement, body=document.body;
const reduce=window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;

try{
  const saved=localStorage.getItem("he-theme");
  if(saved==="dark"||saved==="light") root.dataset.theme=saved;
}catch(_){}

const theme=$(".theme-btn");
theme?.addEventListener("click",()=>{
  const next=root.dataset.theme==="dark"?"light":"dark";
  root.dataset.theme=next;
  try{localStorage.setItem("he-theme",next)}catch(_){}
  toast(next==="dark"?"Modo oscuro activado":"Modo claro activado");
});

const menu=$(".menu-btn"), nav=$(".nav"), backdrop=$(".backdrop");
function closeMenu(){nav?.classList.remove("open");menu?.setAttribute("aria-expanded","false");backdrop?.classList.remove("show")}
menu?.addEventListener("click",()=>{
  const open=!nav?.classList.contains("open");
  nav?.classList.toggle("open",open);menu.setAttribute("aria-expanded",String(open));backdrop?.classList.toggle("show",open);
});
backdrop?.addEventListener("click",closeMenu);
$$(".nav a").forEach(a=>a.addEventListener("click",closeMenu));
window.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.key==="Escape"){closeMenu();$("#he-modal")?.classList.remove("show");backdrop?.classList.remove("show")}});

const progress=document.createElement("div");progress.className="progress-scroll";body.append(progress);
function scrollFX(){
  const max=Math.max(0,document.documentElement.scrollHeight-window.innerHeight);
  progress.style.width=(max?(window.scrollY/max)*100:0)+"%";
  $(".site-header")?.classList.toggle("scrolled",window.scrollY>12);
  $("#topBtn")?.classList.toggle("show",window.scrollY>450);
}
window.addEventListener("scroll",scrollFX,{passive:true});scrollFX();
$("#topBtn")?.addEventListener("click",()=>window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:reduce?"auto":"smooth"}));

const current=(location.pathname.split("/").pop()||"index.html").toLowerCase();
$$(".nav a").forEach(a=>{try{
  const target=(new URL(a.href,location.href).pathname.split("/").pop()||"index.html").toLowerCase();
  a.classList.toggle("active",target===current);
}catch(_){}});

if("IntersectionObserver" in window && !reduce){
  const io=new IntersectionObserver(entries=>entries.forEach(entry=>{
    if(entry.isIntersecting){entry.target.classList.add("show");io.unobserve(entry.target)}
  }),{threshold:.08,rootMargin:"0px 0px -35px"});
  $$(".reveal").forEach((el,i)=>{el.style.transitionDelay=Math.min(i*28,240)+"ms";io.observe(el)});
}else $$(".reveal").forEach(el=>el.classList.add("show"));

if(!reduce && window.matchMedia("(pointer:fine)").matches){
  const glow=document.createElement("div");glow.className="cursor-glow";body.append(glow);
  window.addEventListener("pointermove",e=>{glow.style.left=e.clientX+"px";glow.style.top=e.clientY+"px"},{passive:true});
  $$(".visual[data-tilt='true'],.tilt").forEach(el=>{
    el.addEventListener("pointermove",e=>{
      const r=el.getBoundingClientRect(),x=(e.clientX-r.left)/r.width-.5,y=(e.clientY-r.top)/r.height-.5;
      el.style.transform=`perspective(900px) rotateX(${y*-2.4}deg) rotateY(${x*2.4}deg) translateY(-4px)`;
    });
    el.addEventListener("pointerleave",()=>el.style.transform="");
  });
}

if(!reduce){
  const box=document.createElement("div");box.id="particles";
  const count=innerWidth<600?10:20;
  for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
    const p=document.createElement("i");p.className="particle";
    p.style.cssText=`--s:${2+Math.random()*6}px;--x:${Math.random()*100}%;--o:${.06+Math.random()*.16};--d:${11+Math.random()*15}s;--delay:${-Math.random()*20}s`;
    box.append(p);
  }
  body.prepend(box);
}

function toast(message,ms=3000){
  let stack=$(".toast-stack");
  if(!stack){stack=document.createElement("div");stack.className="toast-stack";stack.style.cssText="position:fixed;right:18px;top:88px;z-index:500;display:grid;gap:9px;width:min(360px,calc(100vw - 36px))";body.append(stack)}
  const t=document.createElement("div");
  t.textContent=message;t.style.cssText="padding:13px 15px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:14px;background:var(--surface-solid);color:var(--text);box-shadow:var(--shadow);font-weight:700";
  stack.append(t);setTimeout(()=>{t.remove()},ms);
}
window.heToast=toast;

$$(".btn").forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener("pointerdown",e=>{
  if(reduce)return;
  const r=document.createElement("span"),q=btn.getBoundingClientRect(),s=Math.max(q.width,q.height);
  r.className="ripple";r.style.width=r.style.height=s+"px";r.style.left=(e.clientX-q.left-s/2)+"px";r.style.top=(e.clientY-q.top-s/2)+"px";
  btn.append(r);
  r.animate([{transform:"scale(0)",opacity:.65},{transform:"scale(2.4)",opacity:0}],{duration:600,easing:"ease-out"}).onfinish=()=>r.remove();
}));

$$('a[href^="#"]').forEach(a=>a.addEventListener("click",e=>{
  const target=$(a.getAttribute("href"));if(target){e.preventDefault();target.scrollIntoView({behavior:reduce?"auto":"smooth"})}
}));

$$('a[target="_blank"]').forEach(a=>a.rel="noopener noreferrer");

$$("[data-search-target]").forEach(input=>input.addEventListener("input",()=>{
  const target=$(input.dataset.searchTarget);if(!target)return;
  const q=input.value.trim().toLowerCase();
  $$("details,article,.card",target).forEach(el=>el.hidden=!!q&&!el.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(q));
}));

function animateCounter(el){
  const n=Number(el.dataset.count);if(!Number.isFinite(n))return;
  if(reduce){el.textContent=n.toLocaleString("es-ES");return}
  const start=performance.now(),duration=900;
  const frame=t=>{
    const p=Math.min(1,(t-start)/duration),ease=1-Math.pow(1-p,3);
    el.textContent=Math.round(n*ease).toLocaleString("es-ES");
    if(p<1)requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  };requestAnimationFrame(frame);
}
if("IntersectionObserver" in window && !reduce){
  const io=new IntersectionObserver(es=>es.forEach(e=>{if(e.isIntersecting){animateCounter(e.target);io.unobserve(e.target)}}),{threshold:.5});
  $$("[data-count]").forEach(e=>io.observe(e));
}else $$("[data-count]").forEach(animateCounter);

function openModal(title,content){
  let m=$("#he-modal");
  if(!m){
    m=document.createElement("section");m.id="he-modal";m.className="modal";m.setAttribute("role","dialog");m.setAttribute("aria-modal","true");
    body.append(m);
  }
  m.innerHTML=`<button class="modal-close" type="button" aria-label="Cerrar">×</button><h2>${title}</h2><div>${content}</div>`;
  m.classList.add("show");backdrop?.classList.add("show");m.querySelector(".modal-close")?.focus();
  m.querySelector(".modal-close")?.addEventListener("click",()=>{m.classList.remove("show");backdrop?.classList.remove("show")},{once:true});
}
window.heModal=openModal;

$$("form").forEach(form=>{
  form.addEventListener("invalid",()=>toast("Revisa los campos marcados antes de continuar."),true);
  form.addEventListener("submit",e=>{
    const btn=form.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
    if(btn && btn.dataset.locked==="1"){e.preventDefault();return}
    if(btn){btn.dataset.locked="1";btn.dataset.original=btn.textContent;btn.textContent="Procesando…";btn.disabled=true;
      setTimeout(()=>{btn.disabled=false;btn.dataset.locked="0";btn.textContent=btn.dataset.original||"Enviar"},1600)}
  });
});

const year=$("#year");if(year)year.textContent=new Date().getFullYear();

try{
  if(!localStorage.getItem("he-cookie-choice")){
    const b=document.createElement("aside");b.className="cookie-banner show";b.setAttribute("aria-label","Preferencias de privacidad");
    b.innerHTML=`<div><strong>Preferencias de privacidad</strong><br><span class="muted">Podemos guardar preferencias básicas como el modo de color en este dispositivo.</span></div><div class="cookie-actions"><button class="btn secondary" type="button" data-cookie="necessary">Continuar</button><button class="btn primary" type="button" data-cookie="ok">Aceptar</button></div>`;
    body.append(b);
    b.addEventListener("click",e=>{const x=e.target.closest("[data-cookie]");if(x){localStorage.setItem("he-cookie-choice",x.dataset.cookie);b.remove();toast("Preferencia guardada ✓")}});
  }
}catch(_){}

window.addEventListener("offline",()=>toast("Sin conexión: algunas funciones pueden esperar."));
window.addEventListener("online",()=>toast("Conexión recuperada ✓"));
window.HuellitasEternas={version:"3.1.0",toast,openModal};
})();
</script>
</body></html>

==================== FIN DE memorial.html ====================

<script>
(()=>{"use strict";
const $=(s,c=document)=>c.querySelector(s), $$=(s,c=document)=>[...c.querySelectorAll(s)];
const root=document.documentElement, body=document.body;
const reduce=window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;

try{
  const saved=localStorage.getItem("he-theme");
  if(saved==="dark"||saved==="light") root.dataset.theme=saved;
}catch(_){}

const theme=$(".theme-btn");
theme?.addEventListener("click",()=>{
  const next=root.dataset.theme==="dark"?"light":"dark";
  root.dataset.theme=next;
  try{localStorage.setItem("he-theme",next)}catch(_){}
  toast(next==="dark"?"Modo oscuro activado":"Modo claro activado");
});

const menu=$(".menu-btn"), nav=$(".nav"), backdrop=$(".backdrop");
function closeMenu(){nav?.classList.remove("open");menu?.setAttribute("aria-expanded","false");backdrop?.classList.remove("show")}
menu?.addEventListener("click",()=>{
  const open=!nav?.classList.contains("open");
  nav?.classList.toggle("open",open);menu.setAttribute("aria-expanded",String(open));backdrop?.classList.toggle("show",open);
});
backdrop?.addEventListener("click",closeMenu);
$$(".nav a").forEach(a=>a.addEventListener("click",closeMenu));
window.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.key==="Escape"){closeMenu();$("#he-modal")?.classList.remove("show");backdrop?.classList.remove("show")}});

const progress=document.createElement("div");progress.className="progress-scroll";body.append(progress);
function scrollFX(){
  const max=Math.max(0,document.documentElement.scrollHeight-window.innerHeight);
  progress.style.width=(max?(window.scrollY/max)*100:0)+"%";
  $(".site-header")?.classList.toggle("scrolled",window.scrollY>12);
  $("#topBtn")?.classList.toggle("show",window.scrollY>450);
}
window.addEventListener("scroll",scrollFX,{passive:true});scrollFX();
$("#topBtn")?.addEventListener("click",()=>window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:reduce?"auto":"smooth"}));

const current=(location.pathname.split("/").pop()||"index.html").toLowerCase();
$$(".nav a").forEach(a=>{try{
  const target=(new URL(a.href,location.href).pathname.split("/").pop()||"index.html").toLowerCase();
  a.classList.toggle("active",target===current);
}catch(_){}});

if("IntersectionObserver" in window && !reduce){
  const io=new IntersectionObserver(entries=>entries.forEach(entry=>{
    if(entry.isIntersecting){entry.target.classList.add("show");io.unobserve(entry.target)}
  }),{threshold:.08,rootMargin:"0px 0px -35px"});
  $$(".reveal").forEach((el,i)=>{el.style.transitionDelay=Math.min(i*28,240)+"ms";io.observe(el)});
}else $$(".reveal").forEach(el=>el.classList.add("show"));

if(!reduce && window.matchMedia("(pointer:fine)").matches){
  const glow=document.createElement("div");glow.className="cursor-glow";body.append(glow);
  window.addEventListener("pointermove",e=>{glow.style.left=e.clientX+"px";glow.style.top=e.clientY+"px"},{passive:true});
  $$(".visual[data-tilt='true'],.tilt").forEach(el=>{
    el.addEventListener("pointermove",e=>{
      const r=el.getBoundingClientRect(),x=(e.clientX-r.left)/r.width-.5,y=(e.clientY-r.top)/r.height-.5;
      el.style.transform=`perspective(900px) rotateX(${y*-2.4}deg) rotateY(${x*2.4}deg) translateY(-4px)`;
    });
    el.addEventListener("pointerleave",()=>el.style.transform="");
  });
}

if(!reduce){
  const box=document.createElement("div");box.id="particles";
  const count=innerWidth<600?10:20;
  for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
    const p=document.createElement("i");p.className="particle";
    p.style.cssText=`--s:${2+Math.random()*6}px;--x:${Math.random()*100}%;--o:${.06+Math.random()*.16};--d:${11+Math.random()*15}s;--delay:${-Math.random()*20}s`;
    box.append(p);
  }
  body.prepend(box);
}

function toast(message,ms=3000){
  let stack=$(".toast-stack");
  if(!stack){stack=document.createElement("div");stack.className="toast-stack";stack.style.cssText="position:fixed;right:18px;top:88px;z-index:500;display:grid;gap:9px;width:min(360px,calc(100vw - 36px))";body.append(stack)}
  const t=document.createElement("div");
  t.textContent=message;t.style.cssText="padding:13px 15px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:14px;background:var(--surface-solid);color:var(--text);box-shadow:var(--shadow);font-weight:700";
  stack.append(t);setTimeout(()=>{t.remove()},ms);
}
window.heToast=toast;

$$(".btn").forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener("pointerdown",e=>{
  if(reduce)return;
  const r=document.createElement("span"),q=btn.getBoundingClientRect(),s=Math.max(q.width,q.height);
  r.className="ripple";r.style.width=r.style.height=s+"px";r.style.left=(e.clientX-q.left-s/2)+"px";r.style.top=(e.clientY-q.top-s/2)+"px";
  btn.append(r);
  r.animate([{transform:"scale(0)",opacity:.65},{transform:"scale(2.4)",opacity:0}],{duration:600,easing:"ease-out"}).onfinish=()=>r.remove();
}));

$$('a[href^="#"]').forEach(a=>a.addEventListener("click",e=>{
  const target=$(a.getAttribute("href"));if(target){e.preventDefault();target.scrollIntoView({behavior:reduce?"auto":"smooth"})}
}));

$$('a[target="_blank"]').forEach(a=>a.rel="noopener noreferrer");

$$("[data-search-target]").forEach(input=>input.addEventListener("input",()=>{
  const target=$(input.dataset.searchTarget);if(!target)return;
  const q=input.value.trim().toLowerCase();
  $$("details,article,.card",target).forEach(el=>el.hidden=!!q&&!el.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(q));
}));

function animateCounter(el){
  const n=Number(el.dataset.count);if(!Number.isFinite(n))return;
  if(reduce){el.textContent=n.toLocaleString("es-ES");return}
  const start=performance.now(),duration=900;
  const frame=t=>{
    const p=Math.min(1,(t-start)/duration),ease=1-Math.pow(1-p,3);
    el.textContent=Math.round(n*ease).toLocaleString("es-ES");
    if(p<1)requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  };requestAnimationFrame(frame);
}
if("IntersectionObserver" in window && !reduce){
  const io=new IntersectionObserver(es=>es.forEach(e=>{if(e.isIntersecting){animateCounter(e.target);io.unobserve(e.target)}}),{threshold:.5});
  $$("[data-count]").forEach(e=>io.observe(e));
}else $$("[data-count]").forEach(animateCounter);

function openModal(title,content){
  let m=$("#he-modal");
  if(!m){
    m=document.createElement("section");m.id="he-modal";m.className="modal";m.setAttribute("role","dialog");m.setAttribute("aria-modal","true");
    body.append(m);
  }
  m.innerHTML=`<button class="modal-close" type="button" aria-label="Cerrar">×</button><h2>${title}</h2><div>${content}</div>`;
  m.classList.add("show");backdrop?.classList.add("show");m.querySelector(".modal-close")?.focus();
  m.querySelector(".modal-close")?.addEventListener("click",()=>{m.classList.remove("show");backdrop?.classList.remove("show")},{once:true});
}
window.heModal=openModal;

$$("form").forEach(form=>{
  form.addEventListener("invalid",()=>toast("Revisa los campos marcados antes de continuar."),true);
  form.addEventListener("submit",e=>{
    const btn=form.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
    if(btn && btn.dataset.locked==="1"){e.preventDefault();return}
    if(btn){btn.dataset.locked="1";btn.dataset.original=btn.textContent;btn.textContent="Procesando…";btn.disabled=true;
      setTimeout(()=>{btn.disabled=false;btn.dataset.locked="0";btn.textContent=btn.dataset.original||"Enviar"},1600)}
  });
});

const year=$("#year");if(year)year.textContent=new Date().getFullYear();

try{
  if(!localStorage.getItem("he-cookie-choice")){
    const b=document.createElement("aside");b.className="cookie-banner show";b.setAttribute("aria-label","Preferencias de privacidad");
    b.innerHTML=`<div><strong>Preferencias de privacidad</strong><br><span class="muted">Podemos guardar preferencias básicas como el modo de color en este dispositivo.</span></div><div class="cookie-actions"><button class="btn secondary" type="button" data-cookie="necessary">Continuar</button><button class="btn primary" type="button" data-cookie="ok">Aceptar</button></div>`;
    body.append(b);
    b.addEventListener("click",e=>{const x=e.target.closest("[data-cookie]");if(x){localStorage.setItem("he-cookie-choice",x.dataset.cookie);b.remove();toast("Preferencia guardada ✓")}});
  }
}catch(_){}

window.addEventListener("offline",()=>toast("Sin conexión: algunas funciones pueden esperar."));
window.addEventListener("online",()=>toast("Conexión recuperada ✓"));
window.HuellitasEternas={version:"3.1.0",toast,openModal};
})();
</script>
</body></html>

==================== FIN DE como-funciona.html ====================

  <div class="container copyright">© <span id="year"></span> Huellitas Eternas · Hecho con cariño.</div>

<script>
(()=>{"use strict";
const $=(s,c=document)=>c.querySelector(s), $$=(s,c=document)=>[...c.querySelectorAll(s)];
const root=document.documentElement, body=document.body;
const reduce=window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;

try{
  const saved=localStorage.getItem("he-theme");
  if(saved==="dark"||saved==="light") root.dataset.theme=saved;
}catch(_){}

const theme=$(".theme-btn");
theme?.addEventListener("click",()=>{
  const next=root.dataset.theme==="dark"?"light":"dark";
  root.dataset.theme=next;
  try{localStorage.setItem("he-theme",next)}catch(_){}
  toast(next==="dark"?"Modo oscuro activado":"Modo claro activado");
});

const menu=$(".menu-btn"), nav=$(".nav"), backdrop=$(".backdrop");
function closeMenu(){nav?.classList.remove("open");menu?.setAttribute("aria-expanded","false");backdrop?.classList.remove("show")}
menu?.addEventListener("click",()=>{
  const open=!nav?.classList.contains("open");
  nav?.classList.toggle("open",open);menu.setAttribute("aria-expanded",String(open));backdrop?.classList.toggle("show",open);
});
backdrop?.addEventListener("click",closeMenu);
$$(".nav a").forEach(a=>a.addEventListener("click",closeMenu));
window.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.key==="Escape"){closeMenu();$("#he-modal")?.classList.remove("show");backdrop?.classList.remove("show")}});

const progress=document.createElement("div");progress.className="progress-scroll";body.append(progress);
function scrollFX(){
  const max=Math.max(0,document.documentElement.scrollHeight-window.innerHeight);
  progress.style.width=(max?(window.scrollY/max)*100:0)+"%";
  $(".site-header")?.classList.toggle("scrolled",window.scrollY>12);
  $("#topBtn")?.classList.toggle("show",window.scrollY>450);
}
window.addEventListener("scroll",scrollFX,{passive:true});scrollFX();
$("#topBtn")?.addEventListener("click",()=>window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:reduce?"auto":"smooth"}));

const current=(location.pathname.split("/").pop()||"index.html").toLowerCase();
$$(".nav a").forEach(a=>{try{
  const target=(new URL(a.href,location.href).pathname.split("/").pop()||"index.html").toLowerCase();
  a.classList.toggle("active",target===current);
}catch(_){}});

if("IntersectionObserver" in window && !reduce){
  const io=new IntersectionObserver(entries=>entries.forEach(entry=>{
    if(entry.isIntersecting){entry.target.classList.add("show");io.unobserve(entry.target)}
  }),{threshold:.08,rootMargin:"0px 0px -35px"});
  $$(".reveal").forEach((el,i)=>{el.style.transitionDelay=Math.min(i*28,240)+"ms";io.observe(el)});
}else $$(".reveal").forEach(el=>el.classList.add("show"));

if(!reduce && window.matchMedia("(pointer:fine)").matches){
  const glow=document.createElement("div");glow.className="cursor-glow";body.append(glow);
  window.addEventListener("pointermove",e=>{glow.style.left=e.clientX+"px";glow.style.top=e.clientY+"px"},{passive:true});
  $$(".visual[data-tilt='true'],.tilt").forEach(el=>{
    el.addEventListener("pointermove",e=>{
      const r=el.getBoundingClientRect(),x=(e.clientX-r.left)/r.width-.5,y=(e.clientY-r.top)/r.height-.5;
      el.style.transform=`perspective(900px) rotateX(${y*-2.4}deg) rotateY(${x*2.4}deg) translateY(-4px)`;
    });
    el.addEventListener("pointerleave",()=>el.style.transform="");
  });
}

if(!reduce){
  const box=document.createElement("div");box.id="particles";
  const count=innerWidth<600?10:20;
  for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
    const p=document.createElement("i");p.className="particle";
    p.style.cssText=`--s:${2+Math.random()*6}px;--x:${Math.random()*100}%;--o:${.06+Math.random()*.16};--d:${11+Math.random()*15}s;--delay:${-Math.random()*20}s`;
    box.append(p);
  }
  body.prepend(box);
}

function toast(message,ms=3000){
  let stack=$(".toast-stack");
  if(!stack){stack=document.createElement("div");stack.className="toast-stack";stack.style.cssText="position:fixed;right:18px;top:88px;z-index:500;display:grid;gap:9px;width:min(360px,calc(100vw - 36px))";body.append(stack)}
  const t=document.createElement("div");
  t.textContent=message;t.style.cssText="padding:13px 15px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:14px;background:var(--surface-solid);color:var(--text);box-shadow:var(--shadow);font-weight:700";
  stack.append(t);setTimeout(()=>{t.remove()},ms);
}
window.heToast=toast;

$$(".btn").forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener("pointerdown",e=>{
  if(reduce)return;
  const r=document.createElement("span"),q=btn.getBoundingClientRect(),s=Math.max(q.width,q.height);
  r.className="ripple";r.style.width=r.style.height=s+"px";r.style.left=(e.clientX-q.left-s/2)+"px";r.style.top=(e.clientY-q.top-s/2)+"px";
  btn.append(r);
  r.animate([{transform:"scale(0)",opacity:.65},{transform:"scale(2.4)",opacity:0}],{duration:600,easing:"ease-out"}).onfinish=()=>r.remove();
}));

$$('a[href^="#"]').forEach(a=>a.addEventListener("click",e=>{
  const target=$(a.getAttribute("href"));if(target){e.preventDefault();target.scrollIntoView({behavior:reduce?"auto":"smooth"})}
}));

$$('a[target="_blank"]').forEach(a=>a.rel="noopener noreferrer");

$$("[data-search-target]").forEach(input=>input.addEventListener("input",()=>{
  const target=$(input.dataset.searchTarget);if(!target)return;
  const q=input.value.trim().toLowerCase();
  $$("details,article,.card",target).forEach(el=>el.hidden=!!q&&!el.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(q));
}));

function animateCounter(el){
  const n=Number(el.dataset.count);if(!Number.isFinite(n))return;
  if(reduce){el.textContent=n.toLocaleString("es-ES");return}
  const start=performance.now(),duration=900;
  const frame=t=>{
    const p=Math.min(1,(t-start)/duration),ease=1-Math.pow(1-p,3);
    el.textContent=Math.round(n*ease).toLocaleString("es-ES");
    if(p<1)requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  };requestAnimationFrame(frame);
}
if("IntersectionObserver" in window && !reduce){
  const io=new IntersectionObserver(es=>es.forEach(e=>{if(e.isIntersecting){animateCounter(e.target);io.unobserve(e.target)}}),{threshold:.5});
  $$("[data-count]").forEach(e=>io.observe(e));
}else $$("[data-count]").forEach(animateCounter);

function openModal(title,content){
  let m=$("#he-modal");
  if(!m){
    m=document.createElement("section");m.id="he-modal";m.className="modal";m.setAttribute("role","dialog");m.setAttribute("aria-modal","true");
    body.append(m);
  }
  m.innerHTML=`<button class="modal-close" type="button" aria-label="Cerrar">×</button><h2>${title}</h2><div>${content}</div>`;
  m.classList.add("show");backdrop?.classList.add("show");m.querySelector(".modal-close")?.focus();
  m.querySelector(".modal-close")?.addEventListener("click",()=>{m.classList.remove("show");backdrop?.classList.remove("show")},{once:true});
}
window.heModal=openModal;

$$("form").forEach(form=>{
  form.addEventListener("invalid",()=>toast("Revisa los campos marcados antes de continuar."),true);
  form.addEventListener("submit",e=>{
    const btn=form.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
    if(btn && btn.dataset.locked==="1"){e.preventDefault();return}
    if(btn){btn.dataset.locked="1";btn.dataset.original=btn.textContent;btn.textContent="Procesando…";btn.disabled=true;
      setTimeout(()=>{btn.disabled=false;btn.dataset.locked="0";btn.textContent=btn.dataset.original||"Enviar"},1600)}
  });
});

const year=$("#year");if(year)year.textContent=new Date().getFullYear();

try{
  if(!localStorage.getItem("he-cookie-choice")){
    const b=document.createElement("aside");b.className="cookie-banner show";b.setAttribute("aria-label","Preferencias de privacidad");
    b.innerHTML=`<div><strong>Preferencias de privacidad</strong><br><span class="muted">Podemos guardar preferencias básicas como el modo de color en este dispositivo.</span></div><div class="cookie-actions"><button class="btn secondary" type="button" data-cookie="necessary">Continuar</button><button class="btn primary" type="button" data-cookie="ok">Aceptar</button></div>`;
    body.append(b);
    b.addEventListener("click",e=>{const x=e.target.closest("[data-cookie]");if(x){localStorage.setItem("he-cookie-choice",x.dataset.cookie);b.remove();toast("Preferencia guardada ✓")}});
  }
}catch(_){}

window.addEventListener("offline",()=>toast("Sin conexión: algunas funciones pueden esperar."));
window.addEventListener("online",()=>toast("Conexión recuperada ✓"));
window.HuellitasEternas={version:"3.1.0",toast,openModal};
})();
